- **holdout-verifier**: You are the holdout verifier. You run a project's holdout scenarios against a running copy of the app, as a user would, and report what actually happened. Only you and the holdout writer may read the scenario file; every other agent is kept out of it. ([coleam00/factory-mission-control](https://github.com/coleam00/factory-mission-control/blob/HEAD/fixtures/pack/sdlc/holdouts/commands/holdout-verifier.md) · ★48)
- **holdout-writer**: You are the holdout writer. You write and extend a project's holdout scenarios: acceptance checks the agents building the code never see, graded by someone else against the product's intent on the code as it will merge. Only you and the holdout verifier may read the scenario file; every other agent is kept out of it. ([coleam00/factory-mission-control](https://github.com/coleam00/factory-mission-control/blob/HEAD/fixtures/pack/sdlc/holdouts/commands/holdout-writer.md) · ★48)

- **hooks**: Manage named hooks and synchronize each receiving Agent's or Git's native configuration. Use the dashboard's **Hooks** page to add, edit, import, enable or disable entries, then preview before syncing. Hooks also appear in destination **Targets** and **Projects**, **Sync**, and **Settings → Backups**. The **Hooks** page works only when the browser opens the dashboard by `localhost`, an IP address ([runkids/skillshare](https://github.com/runkids/skillshare/blob/HEAD/website/docs/reference/commands/hooks.md) · ★3.2k)
- **hooks**: 이름이 있는 hooks를 관리하고 각 Agent의 네이티브 설정에 동기화합니다. Dashboard의 **Hooks**에서 추가, 편집, 가져오기, 활성화／비활성화, 미리보기와 동기화를 할 수 있습니다. **Targets**, **Projects**, **Sync**, **Settings → Backups**에도 표시됩니다. **Hooks** 페이지는 브라우저가 `localhost`, IP 주소 또는 trusted origin으로 대시보드를 열 때만 동작합니다. [리버스 프록시](/docs/reference/commands/ui#reverse-proxy)를 참고하세요. ([runkids/skillshare](https://github.com/runkids/skillshare/blob/HEAD/website/i18n/ko/docusaurus-plugin-content-docs/current/reference/commands/hooks.md) · ★3.2k)
- **hooks**: 管理具名 hooks，将各 Agent 的原生定义同步到配置文件。Dashboard 的 **Hooks** 提供新增、编辑、导入、启用／停用、预览与同步；“目标”、“项目”、“同步”及“设置 → 备份”也有对应入口。**Hooks** 页面只有在浏览器通过 `localhost`、IP 地址或 trusted origin 打开 dashboard 时才能工作，参见 [Reverse Proxy](/docs/reference/commands/ui#reverse-proxy)。 ([runkids/skillshare](https://github.com/runkids/skillshare/blob/HEAD/website/i18n/zh-Hans/docusaurus-plugin-content-docs/current/reference/commands/hooks.md) · ★3.2k)
- **hooks**: 管理具名 hooks，將各 Agent 的原生定義同步至設定檔。Dashboard 的 **Hooks** 提供新增、編輯、匯入、啟用／停用、預覽與同步；「目標」、「專案」、「同步」及「設定 → 備份」也有對應入口。**Hooks** 頁面只有在瀏覽器以 `localhost`、IP 位址或 trusted origin 開啟 dashboard 時才能運作，參見 [Reverse Proxy](/docs/reference/commands/ui#reverse-proxy)。 ([runkids/skillshare](https://github.com/runkids/skillshare/blob/HEAD/website/i18n/zh-Hant/docusaurus-plugin-content-docs/current/reference/commands/hooks.md) · ★3.2k)
- **hooks**: 名前付き hooks を管理し、各 Agent のネイティブ設定に同期します。Dashboard の **Hooks** で追加、編集、インポート、有効／無効、プレビュー、同期を行えます。**Targets**、**Projects**、**Sync**、**Settings → Backups** にも表示されます。**Hooks** ページは、ブラウザが `localhost`、IP アドレス、または trusted origin でダッシュボードを開いている場合にのみ動作します。[リバースプロキシ](/docs/reference/commands/ui#reverse-proxy) を参照してください。 ([runkids/skillshare](https://github.com/runkids/skillshare/blob/HEAD/website/i18n/ja/docusaurus-plugin-content-docs/current/reference/commands/hooks.md) · ★3.2k)
- **hooks**: Install, uninstall, or inspect OpenLoomi's Claude Code hooks ([melandlabs/openloomi](https://github.com/melandlabs/openloomi/blob/HEAD/plugins/claude/commands/hooks.md) · ★1k · 2 copies)
- **hooks**: Discovers traditional hook scripts in the resolved hooks directory (`core.hooksPath` when set, otherwise `.git/hooks`). A relative `core.hooksPath` is relative to the directory hooks run in (the top of the work tree, or the git directory in a bare repository), so the same hooks are found from any subdirectory. Configured `hook.&lt;name&gt;.*` aliases are resolved when a hook runs, not in the list. ([gitbutlerapp/grit](https://github.com/gitbutlerapp/grit/blob/HEAD/content/docs/commands/hooks.md) · ★598)
- **hooks**: `libra hooks` 的目标是为 `libra agent enable` 安装的 AI provider hook 配置提供隐藏兼容入口。它不作为普通用户主命令推广，主要用于帮助文本、测试和 agent hook 配置的稳定性。它不是 Git hooks bridge：不读取 `.git/hooks`，不读取 `core.hooksPath`，也不把 stock Git hook 生命周期纳入 Libra 核心能力；该决策见 [`_compatibility.md` D3](_compatibility.md#d3git-hooks-bridge-作为核心特性)。 ([libra-tools/libra](https://github.com/libra-tools/libra/blob/HEAD/docs/development/commands/hooks.md) · ★101)
- **hooks**: Internal entry point invoked by external AI agent hook configurations that capture lifecycle events (session start, prompt submission, tool use, permissions, sub-agent boundaries, model updates, compaction, stop, and session end) into the Libra session store. Operators almost never type `libra hooks ...` directly — the hook configs installed by `libra agent enable` reference these subcommands.
